- Smooth uses tau from n/4 to n*0.001, eta=0.04 normalized by max grad, projected sum=n. It subtracts mean grad. This is subgradient of LSE over all coefficients. Since many coefficients below max, weighting by exp((c-mc)/tau). Tau~952 initially, very smooth, gradient may point to flatten all coefficients, not targeting max. Later tau~3.8, active. - Hard uses one argmax. It subtracts mean of full gradient vector (zeros outside support). This may oscillate among near max indices.
- Perturb block multiplicative with width small, sigma up to 0.6; after each perturb 15s smooth +10s hard. Could get local.

But we can avoid smooth over all p by using hard/top active gradients: For top K active indices, each gradient is 2 shifted a, no correlation. We can blend active gradients instead of LSE. That is O(K * support length) ~ K*n if naive; but K small (<=50), support length ~n, okay. For K=top 20, update vector weighted sum of shifted a arrays: cost K*n ~200k for n=10k, fine. This is much faster than full smooth correlation. And targets max constraints, not diffuse all coefficients. The previous smooth used LSE all c to handle non-differentiable; top active with softmax weights over top c can do similar. We can choose top K based on c >= M - delta (delta small) or largest top K. Use weights w = softmax((c_top - M)/tau) or just uniform active if near ties. Then gradient = 2∑ w_k shift_k(a) (with each shift vector length n, zero outside). Then project.

This active-set gradient method may be more efficient and precise. Need adaptive K/tau:
- Compute conv c, max M, indices sorted? Sorting 7617 each iter maybe O(n log n) okay but could be cost. Use `np.argpartition` for top K if K small; or threshold.
- We want weights for constraints near max. Let `active = c >= M - tol`, maybe tol = max(1e-12, alpha*M) with alpha small e.g. 0.001. If too many active (e.g., uniform many near max), cap top K=64 using argpartition. Compute w = softmax((c_active - M)/tau_active). tau_active small, like 0.005*M? If many active, weights nearly uniform. This captures critical set.
- Update direction g = 2∑_{k∈active} w_k shift_k(a). Since each shift_k(a) has support where k-i in [0,n-1]. For each k, `js=max(0,k-n+1), je=min(n-1,k)+1; g[js:je] += w_k*2*a[k - np.arange(js,je)]`. For K up to 64 and n=5000, 320k ops, fine.
- Project under sum: subtract mean of g over support? Since constraint sum constant. We can subtract average of g (because any constant vector in gradient has no effect under translation? - `optim_active(a, n, deadline, mode)`: does conv, active set, update. Modes: hard top1, active topK, LSE topK, log update, Euclidean.

Could use "alternating" between active and a penalty for convexity:
Objective F = 2n max c. Equivalent to minimize λ with c_k≤λ. We can use constrained projected subgradient: current λ = max c. For each active k, reduce c_k by moving mass to positions that lower it, while increasing inactive? The active gradient does that.

Line search/backtracking:
At each iteration, compute current obj. Try step with eta; compute new conv; if obj improves, accept; else halve eta. This doubles conv cost, but active update cheap. Could do every 10 iterations or when stalled.

Momentum:
Maintain velocity `v` in Euclidean space. For centering: `g0 = g - mean(g)`. Update `v = beta*v + (1-beta)*g0?` Actually gradient descent: `proposal = a - eta*(beta*v + g0)`? Need maintain on simplex; after projection/renormalize, reset v? Simpler: use Nesterov: `y = a + momentum*(a - prev_a)`; compute gradient at y? But conv expensive. Could maintain update direction: `v = beta*v + g0; a <- proj(a - eta*v)`. Momentum may help. Use beta 0.5-0.9 adapting. Need keep nonnegative; after projection, v not projected maybe. We'll implement simple heavy-ball with decay of negative.

Let's propose final algorithm:
```
def propose_candidate():
    deadline = time.monotonic()+2390 (leave 10s)
    # helper conv_fft, obj, optimize_length
    # generate candidates
    # preflight lengths
    # deep optimize best length
    # periodic perturb/refine
    # final return best sequence exact evaluated

Detailed functions:

def _optimize_sequence(a, n, deadline, tag, max_iters=1000000, eta0=0.08, min_eta=1e-5):
    a=_normalize(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_o=_obj_fft(a,n)
    c=...; k=...
    eta=eta0
    small_reject=0
    for it in ...:
        if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
        # periodically recompute exact? later
        # choose active K/temp adaptively based on it:
        K = 32; tau_frac = 0.05 if it<5000 else 0.01
        g, M = _active_gradient(a, c, K, tau_frac)
        # center
        g = g - g.mean()
        gmax=np.max(np.abs(g))
        if gmax<1e-15: break
        rel = a.mean()/gmax
        step = eta * rel * g
        # maybe line search with eta sequence
        accepted=False
        trial_obj=best? current
        for factor in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625]:
            b = a - step*factor
            b = np.maximum(b,0)
            s=b.sum(); if s<1e-12: break
            b = b/s
            c2=_conv_fft(b,n); o2=2*n*max(c2)
            if o2 < current_o - 1e-12 or it%100==0 and o2<current_o+...:
                 a,b? update best if o2<best_o
                 current_o=o2; c=c2; eta=max(eta*1.1,0.5)? ; accepted=True; break
            else:
                 # maybe if o2 < best, still?
        if not accepted:
             # try log update with small factor? or perturb tiny
             # reduce eta
             eta*=0.8; small_reject+=1
             if small_reject>300: maybe break/perturb
        it%? print
    return best,best_o
```
But we recompute c2 for current after accepted. For next iteration, active gradient uses c. Good.

Top active gradient function:
```
def _active_gradient(a, c, K, tau_frac):
    M=float(np.max(c)); kmax=int(np.argmax(c))
    if M <=0: return zeros, M
    # active threshold
    tol = max(1e-15, tau_frac*M)
    # Try threshold; if many use top K
    active = np.where(c >= M - tol)[0]
    if len(active) > K:
        # choose top K (including max)
        active = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
        # sort maybe not needed
    else:
        # if active exactly one, ok; for weights with all near max, use active
        # If too few? maybe add top 2? not needed.
    if len(active)==1:
        idx=active[0]; g=...
    else:
        z=(c[active]-M)/(max(1e-300, taufrac? maybe tol*0.5))
        z-=np.max(z)
        w=np.exp(z); w/=w.sum()
    g=np.zeros(n)
    for j,k in enumerate(active):
       L=max(0,k-n+1); R=min(n,k+1); if L<R:
          src=k-np.arange(L,R)
          g[L:R] += w[j]*2.0*a[src]
    return g,M
```
But if active = top K by argpartition, weights should also include lower c not selected? We ignore. Good.

`tau` for weights: If we use threshold tol and within active all c≥M-tol, use tau = max(1e-12, tol) maybe. If K=32, if top few have much lower than M, weights small. Could set `tau = max(1e-15, M * max(tau_frac, (M-c[-1])/2)?)` Simpler: `tau = M * max(0.005, tol/M?)` Hmm.

We can choose weights as `w = softmax((c_active - M)/ (M * weight_temp))`, with weight_temp independent 0.005. If active threshold 0.05M, differences up to 0.05M, weights from exp(-10) for bottom if temp 0.005, so top few. If want all top K relevant, temp=0.05. We can pass `weight_temp`. For smoothness use weight_temp=0.02 and K=64.

def _perturb(a, n, rng, strength):
    b=a.copy()
    mode=rng.integers(0,3)
    if mode==0: block multiplicative: w=rng.choice([n//200,n//50,n//10,n//20]); start; b[start:start+w] *= exp(rng.normal(0,strength,size=w)); normalize
    if mode==1: global low-freq: k=rng.integers(1,5); phase=rng.uniform(0,2π); b*=exp(strength*rng? sin); normalize
    if mode==2: log blend with snapshot? Need pass snapshot.

a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-12: a=np.ones(n); else a=a/s

`_run_length` algorithm:
```
def _run_length(n, end_time, parent_seq, init_seq=None, tag=''):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(1234+n)
    if init_seq is not None and len==n: a=normalize(init_seq)
    else: a=resample parent if parent else lowfreq random
    best=a; best_o=objective
    snapshot=best.copy()
    last_perturb = now+20
    stall=0
    mode_cycle=0
    eta=0.08
    reject=0
    while now < end_time - 10:
        if now-last_perturb > perturb_interval and best_o not improved long:
             # perform perturbation from snapshot/best, then quick optimize? In loop we'll just set a=perturb(best) and continue.
        # choose K/temp based on progress: elapsed fraction
        g = _active_gradient(a, c, K, tau)
        # update step; line search.
        # if accepted and o<best_o: update best, snapshot occasionally, reduce? else reject
        # if many rejects: apply perturbation (small) and reset eta
    # final exact check maybe
    return best, exact_score(best)
```
Need compute c once per iteration. We can maintain c current at end. On perturbation, recompute.

Line search details in code:
```
cur_o = _obj(c,n)
# Precompute dirv
g = _active_gradient(a,c,K,wt)
g = g - g.mean()

def _blend_log(a,b,theta):
   la=np.log(a+1e-15); lb=np.log(b+1e-15); lc=theta*la+(1-theta)*lb; return normalize(exp(lc-min(lc)))

Let's write `_active_gradient` carefully:
```
def _active_gradient(a, c, K, weight_frac):
    n = len(a)
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    if mc <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 0
    # top indices
    m = len(c)
    K = int(K)
    if K <= 1:
       idx = int(np.argmax(c))
       return _grad_one(a, idx), mc, idx
    if K >= m:
       active = np.arange(m)
       # maybe if huge, still K=m; but m=2n-1; use top? K cap 128
    else:
       # argpartition
       active = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    # weights
    ca = c[active]
    # if only one distinct? weight uniform?
    denom = max(1e-300, mc * weight_frac)
    z = (ca - mc) / denom
    z = z - np.max(z)
    # avoid underflow: clip z >= -50
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = e.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
       e = np.full_like(e, 1.0)
    w = e / s
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    # loop
    for ii in range(len(active)):
       k = int(active[ii]); tw = 2.0 * float(w[ii])
       if k < n:
           end = k + 1
           # source view a[:end][::-1]
           g[:end] += tw * a[:end][::-1]
       else:
           L = k - n + 1
           # source a[n-1 : L-1 : -1]
           g[L:n] += tw * a[n-1:L-1:-1]
    return g, mc, int(np.argmax(c))
```
Check for k=n? L=1, slice `a[n-1:0:-1]` ok length n-1. For k=2n-2, L=n-1, slice `a[n-1:n-2:-1]` length1. For n=1? We will not use n=1 maybe, but handle: N? If n=1, conv len1, active gradient: k=0<n, g[:1]+=2w a[:1][::-1]. Good.

Potential issue: For k < n, `a[:end][::-1]` creates reversed view each; if end large. Good. For k>=n, `a[n-1:L-1:-1]`. If L=0? For k=n-1, branch <n. Good.

Could sort active by weight descending to maybe early? Not needed.

`_grad_one` maybe not necessary; `_active_gradient` handles K=1 via argpartition? We can special.

`_conv_fft` performance and numerical:
```
def _conv_fft(a, N):
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N)
    m = 2*len(a)-1
    c = c[:m]
    # remove negative roundoff
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c
```
Multiplying complex F by itself: `F * F` maybe yields complex with small imaginary; `irfft` takes complex and assumes conjugate symmetry; due to roundoff imag maybe not perfectly anti; okay. To be safe, use `F.real*F.real + F.imag*F.imag` as real H? But `irfft` expects Hermitian complex; if we pass real array, it treats as complex? We can create `H = F * F; H.imag -= H.imag?` Actually F*F is Hermitian exactly if F is? For real input, F[-k]=conj(F[k]), so F*F at -k = conj(F[k])^2? Wait (conj(F_k))^2 is not conj(F_k^2). If F_k = r e^{iθ}, conj(F_k)^2 = r^2 e^{-2iθ}; conj(F_k^2)=r^2 e^{-2iθ}. Yes Hermitian. Good.
But `irfft` of F*F gives circular convolution of a with a? The DFT of convolution is F*F (not F*conj F). For linear convolution of a with a, DFT of zero-padded linear conv is F*F. Since a real, F*F okay. However, F*F can have imaginary part; `irfft` of Hermitian F*F returns real. Good. Note `np.fft.irfft` expects input satisfying Hermitian symmetry; F*F does. Good.

Need pass N power-of-two >= 2n-1. For n=1, N=1? `bit_length` of 1? compute:
```
def _next_power_of_two(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```
For L=2n-1. If L=7617, N=8192.

`_obj` returns `2.0 * n * float(np.max(c))` if sum =1. If a not normalized exactly due to clip, use `S=a.sum()`? We can pass S for safety: objective = 2n max(c over normalized? If c from a and S not1, score uses S^2. But we maintain sum1. For internal, use `2n max(c)/(S*S)` maybe robust. Let's implement `_obj_from_seq(a,c)` using S = a.sum(); if S<=0 inf. But S approx1. This handles if not normalized.
```
def _obj(a,c):
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return np.inf
    return 2.0*len(a)*float(np.max(c))/(s*s)
```
This is scale invariant. For active gradient, we assume a sum1 for relative step `a.mean()`. If sum not1, mean still fine. Use s in `_normalize`.

`_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s < 1e-12: return np.ones(len(a), dtype=np.float64)/len(a)
    return a/s
```
Maybe if elements huge, sum inf? We avoid.

`_optimize_length` detailed code:
```
def _optimize_length(n, end_time, parent, init=None, tag=''):
    start_t = time.monotonic()
    total = max(1.0, end_time - start_t)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345 + n)
    if init is not None and len(init)==n:
        a=_normalize(init)
    elif parent is not None:
        a=_resample(parent,n)
    else:
        a=_lowfreq_init(n,rng,1)
    N=_next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
    c=_conv_fft(a,N)
    cur_o=_obj(a,c)
    best=a.copy(); best_o=cur_o; best_c=c.copy()
    snapshot=best.copy()
    eta=0.08
    it=0; no_improve=0; last_improve=time.monotonic(); last_print=start_t; last_perturb=start_t
    print(f"[{tag}] init n={n} obj={cur_o:.6f}")
    while time.monotonic() < end_time - 5:
       it +=1
       # schedule K/temp
       frac = min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-start_t)/max(1.0,total))
       if frac < 0.15:
          K=128; wt=0.04
       elif frac<0.45:
          K=64; wt=0.015
       elif frac<0.8:
          K=32; wt=0.006
       else:
          K=16; wt=0.002
       g, mc, kmax = _active_gradient(a,c,K,wt)
       if mc <=0: break
       gc = g - g.mean()
       gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(gc)))
       cur_o = _obj(a,c) # maybe S
       # perturb if stalled
       if (time.monotonic()-last_improve > 45.0) or no_improve > 4000:
           strength = 0.05 + 0.25*min(1.0, (no_improve/5000.0))
           if np.random? use rng
           if rng.random() < 0.35 and snapshot is not None:
                theta=float(rng.uniform(0.3,0.8))
                a = _blend_log(best, snapshot, theta)
           else:
                a = _perturb(a, snapshot, rng, strength)
           a = _normalize(a)
           c = _conv_fft(a,N)
           cur_o = _obj(a,c)
           if cur_o < best_o: update? after perturbation unlikely but check
           eta = 0.08
           no_improve=0
           last_improve=time.monotonic()? perturbation reset, but not improvement. Maybe set last_perturb.
           continue
       # If gradient too small break?
       if gmax < 1e-16: break
       mean_a = float(a.mean())
       dirv = gc * (mean_a / gmax)
       improved=False
       use_log = (it % 6 == 0) or (no_improve > 800)
       # Try additive
       for te in (eta, 0.5*eta, 0.25*eta):
          b = a - te * dirv
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)): break
          b = np.maximum(b,0.0)
          s=b.sum()
          if s < 1e-12: continue
          b = b/s
          c2=_conv_fft(b,N)
          o2=_obj(b,c2)
          if np.isfinite(o2) and (o2 < cur_o - 1e-12):
              a=b; c=c2; cur_o=o2
              eta = min(0.5, te*1.05)
              no_improve=0
              if cur_o < best_o - 1e-10:
                  best_o=cur_o; best=a.copy(); best_c=c2.copy(); last_improve=time.monotonic()
                  if time.monotonic() - last_snapshot > 30: snapshot=best.copy(); last_snapshot=time.monotonic()
              improved=True
              break
       if not improved and use_log:
          # multiplicative with dimensionless h maybe g/gmax centered
          h = (g - g.mean()) / gmax
          h = np.clip(h, -3.0, 3.0)
          for te in (eta, 0.5*eta, 0.25*eta):
              b = a * np.exp(-te * h)
              if not finite: break
              s=b.sum(); if s<1e-12: continue
              b=b/s
              c2=_conv_fft(b,N); o2=_obj(b,c2)
              if finite and o2 < cur_o - 1e-12:
                  a=b; c=c2; cur_o=o2; eta=min(0.5, te*1.05); no_improve=0
                  if cur_o < best_o -1e-10: update best/snapshot
                  improved=True; break
       if not improved:
          eta = max(eta*0.75, 1e-4)
          no_improve += 1
       # print every ~3s
       if time.monotonic() - last_print > 3.0:
           print(f"[{tag}] it={it} obj={cur_o:.6f} best={best_o:.6f} eta={eta:.3f} K={K}")
           last_print=time.monotonic()
    # final maybe one small local from best
    # Check exact? We'll return; maybe run short final polish on best for 20s? The loop already.
    return best, best_o
```

Initialization styles. Let's implement `_lowfreq_init(n, rng, seed_style, parent=None)`:
```
def _lowfreq_init(n, rng, style=1, parent=None):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    if parent is not None:
       a=_resample(parent,n)
       # add small noise lowfreq
       noise = sum(rng.normal(0,0.05,...) sin)??
       a *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.02,size=n)?)
       return _normalize(a)
    if style==0: return ones/n
    # Generate random smooth nonnegative spectrum
    K=6
    f=np.ones(n)
    amp=1.0
    for _ in range(K):
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, 7))
        phase = rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi)
        g = amp * rng.uniform(0.1,0.5) * np.sin(2*math.pi*freq*x/n + phase)
        f = f + g
        amp *= 0.5
    f = np.maximum(f, 1e-3)
    return _normalize(f)
```

Compute `_lse_gradient`:
```
def _lse_gradient(a, c, tau_frac):
    n=len(a); mc=max(c); tau = max(1e-300, tau_frac*mc)
    z=(c-mc)/tau; z-=np.max(z); p=np.exp(z); p/=p.sum()
    N2 = _next_power_of_two(3*n-3) # could be 2* conv? For n=1, 0?
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N2)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N2)
    h = np.fft.irfft(P * np.conj(A), N2)
    h = h[:n] * 2.0
    return h
```
Check for small n, N2 maybe if 3n-3=0 for n=1 =>1.
Need memory: N2 up to next pow2 3n. For n=8000 ->32768, P/A complex 8192? rfft length 32768 =>16385 complex, fine.

Could reuse A from conv? Conv uses N=next pow2 2n, not N2. Different.

Perturb function should not depend on snapshot? Implement:
```
def _perturb(a, snap, rng, strength):
    n=len(a); b=a.copy()
    r = rng.random()
    if r < 0.33 and snap is not None:
        theta = float(rng.uniform(0.4, 0.9))
        return _blend_log(a, snap, theta)
    if r < 0.66:
       # block multiplicative
       w = int(max(2, min(n, n * float(rng.choice([0.01,0.03,0.08,0.15,0.3])))))
       s = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n-w+1)))
       b[s:s+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength, size=w))
    else:
       # lowfreq additive log
       k = int(rng.integers(1, 10))
       phase = float(rng.uniform(0, 2*math.pi))
       x = np.arange(n)
       b *= np.exp(strength * 0.5 * np.sin(2*math.pi*k*x/n + phase))
    return _normalize(b)
```
Need `rng.choice` with list of floats returns numpy scalar; float() okay. If n small. `strength` 0.05-0.4.

Log blend:
```
def _blend_log(a,b,theta):
    ea=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); eb=...; ec=theta*ea+(1-theta)*eb; ec-=ec.max(); out=np.exp(ec); return _normalize(out)
```

Lowfreq init if no parent:
```
def _random_lowfreq(n, rng):
   x=np.arange(n)
   f=1.0 + np.zeros(n)
   for freq in [1,2,3,4,5]:
       amp = rng.uniform(0.05,0.25)
       phase = rng.uniform(0,2π)
       f += amp*np.sin(2π*freq*x/n + phase)
   f = np.maximum(f, 0.05)
   # add small multiplicative noise
   f *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.02,size=n))
   return _normalize(f)
```

```
def _line_search(a, c, n, N, g, eta, use_log):
    cur_o = _obj(a,c)
    mean_a = float(a.mean())
    gc = g - g.mean()
    gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(gc)))
    if gmax < 1e-18:
        return False, a, c, cur_o, eta, None, 0
    dirv = gc * (mean_a / gmax)
    # additive
    for te in (eta, 0.5*eta, 0.25*eta):
       b = a - te*dirv
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)): continue
       b = np.maximum(b,0)
       s=b.sum()
       if s<1e-12: continue
       b/=s
       c2=_conv_fft(b,N); o2=_obj(b,c2)
       if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < cur_o - 1e-10:
            return True,b,c2,o2, min(0.5, te*1.05), 'add', te
    if use_log:
       h = gc/gmax
       h = np.clip(h, -3.0, 3.0)
       for te in (eta, 0.5*eta, 0.25*eta):
          b = a * np.exp(-te*h)
          ...
          if improve: return ...
    return False,a,c,cur_o, max(eta*0.75,1e-5), 'none', 0
```

Implement `_next_power_of_two(m)`:
```
def _next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```
For m=8192, returns 8192.

`_conv_fft(a, N)`:
```
def _conv_fft(a, N):
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N)
    n = len(a)
    c = c[:2*n-1]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c
```
`F*F`: If F complex, product may have -0 imag. `irfft` output small negative; max clip.

`_obj(a,c)`:
```
def _obj(a,c):
    n=len(a)
    s=a.sum()
    if s < 1e-12: return float('inf')
    return 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c)) / (s*s)
```
If c all zeros, max0 ->0.

`_normalize` as above.

`_resample(parent,n)`:
```
def _resample(parent,n):
    if parent is None:
       return np.ones(n)/n
    p=np.asarray(parent,dtype=float)
    p=np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p=p[p>0]? Actually keep zeros.
    p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
    if p.size == n:
       a=p.copy()
    else:
       if p.size == 0: return ones
       x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,p.size)
       x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
       a=np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    return _normalize(a)
```
If parent is length 1? interpolate? `np.linspace` size1 okay? `np.interp` with xp length1 may error? It requires xp increasing; one point? Actually length 1 okay? May return edge value. To be safe if p.size==1 fill with p[0]. But n>=2048.

`_random_lowfreq(n,rng)`:
Use `x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)`. Generate smooth noise.
```
f = np.ones(n)
for _ in range(5):
   freq = int(rng.integers(1, 8))
   phase = float(rng.uniform(0, 2*math.pi))
   amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.25))
   f += amp * np.sin(2*math.pi*freq*x/n + phase)
# optional high-freq small
f *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, size=n))
f = np.maximum(f, 1e-3)
return _normalize(f)
```
Could min go negative if amps sum >1? max sum 1.25, yes can negative; clip to 1e-3. Ensure not zero all.

`_analytic_init(n, rng)`:
```
def _analytic_init(n, rng):
    N = _next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    best = _(random_lowfreq)
    best_obj = _obj(best,_conv_fft(best,N))
    ks = [1,2,3,4,5]
    alphas = [0.2,0.4,0.6,0.8]
    phases = [0.0, math.pi/2, math.pi, 3*math.pi/2]
    for k in ks:
      for alpha in alphas:
        for phase in phases:
           f=1.0 + alpha*np.sin(2*math.pi*k*x/n + phase)
           if np.min(f) <= 0.01: continue
           a=_normalize(f)
           o=_obj(a,_conv_fft(a,N))
           if o < best_obj: best=a; best_obj=o
    # also add a second harmonic? maybe combinations
    for k1 in [1,2,3]:
      k2=k1+1
      for a1 in [0.3,0.5]:
         for a2 in [0.1,0.2]:
            f=1+a1*sin(...k1...)+a2*cos(...k2...)
            if min>0: eval
    return best
```
80 convs maybe 0.1s, okay. But if called only when no parent. For parent, we can skip analytic. Could also call for diversity? Stage A from parent. Stage B from resampled parent; analytic not. If no parent, good.

Maybe if parent is all ones (fallback), analytic useful. We can decide: if parent is None or parent uniform-ish? Simpler: if parent None use analytic; else resample parent. But if parent all ones, resample uniform and active may stall; we can add small perturbation. In `_optimize_length` init from parent: after resample, multiply by exp(normal 0.02) to break symmetry. def _optimize_length(n, end_time, parent, init, tag='', smooth_frac=0.20):
    start_t = time.monotonic()
    end_time = float(end_time)
    total = end_time - start_t
    if total < 20.0:
        if init is not None and len(init)==n: a=_normalize(init)
        elif parent is not None: a=_resample(parent,n)
        else: a=_analytic_init(n, np.random.default_rng(n))
        c=_conv_fft(a,_next_power_of_two(2*n-1))
        return a, _obj(a,c)
    phase1_end = start_t + smooth_frac*total
    N1 = _next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
    N2 = _next_power_of_two(3*n-2)
    ...
```

`_line_search` returns tuple of 7 values; maybe too many but okay. Let's define:
```
def _line_search(a, c, n, N, g, eta, use_log):
    cur_o = _obj(a,c)
    gc = g - g.mean()
    gmax = np.max(np.abs(gc))
    if gmax < 1e-18:
       return False, a, c, cur_o, max(eta*0.75,1e-5), 'none', 0.0
    mean_a = float(a.mean())
    dirv = gc * (mean_a / float(gmax))
    for te in (eta, 0.5*eta, 0.25*eta):
       b = a - te * dirv
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)): continue
       b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
       s = float(b.sum())
       if s < 1e-12: continue
       b = b / s
       c2 = _conv_fft(b, N)
       o2 = _obj(b, c2)
       if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < cur_o - 1e-10:
           return True, b, c2, o2, min(0.5, te*1.05), 'add', te
    if use_log:
       h = gc / float(gmax)
       h = np.clip(h, -3.0, 3.0)
       for te in (eta, 0.5*eta, 0.25*eta):
           b = a * np.exp(-te * h)
           if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)): continue
           s = float(b.sum())
           if s < 1e-12: continue
           b = b / s
           c2 = _conv_fft(b, N)
           o2 = _obj(b, c2)
           if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < cur_o - 1e-10:
               return True, b, c2, o2, min(0.5, te*1.05), 'log', te
    return False, a, c, cur_o, max(eta*0.75, 1e-5), 'none', 0.0
```
Note if `eta` becomes very small 1e-5, step negligible; then no_improve triggers perturb. Good.

def _safe_score(seq):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=float)
        if arr.size == 0: return np.inf
        return float(evaluate_sequence([float(v) for v in arr]))
    except Exception:
        return float('inf')

Let's write code fully here mentally and then final.

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

def _next_power_of_two(m):
    m=int(m)
    if m<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def _normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0:
       return np.array([1.0])
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
       return np.full(a.shape, 1.0/float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a/s

def _conv_fft(a,N):
    F=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)
    m=2*len(a)-1
    c=c[:m]
    np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
    return c

def _obj(a,c):
    n=len(a)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<1e-12: return float('inf')
    return 2.0*float(n)*float(np.max(c))/(s*s)

def _active_gradient(a,c,K,weight_frac):
    n=len(a)
    mc=float(np.max(c))
    if mc<=1e-300:
       return np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    m=len(c)
    K=int(K)
    g=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64)
    if K<=1:
       idx=int(np.argmax(c)); tw=2.0
       k=idx
       if k<n:
          g[:k+1] += tw*a[:k+1][::-1]
       else:
          L=k-n+1
          g[L:n] += tw*a[n-1:L-1:-1]
       return g, mc
    if K>=m:
       active=np.arange(m)
    else:
       active=np.argpartition(c,-K)[-K:]
    ca=c[active]
    denom=max(1e-300, mc*float(weight_frac))
    z=(ca-mc)/denom
    z=z-np.max(z)
    z=np.clip(z,-50.0,0.0)
    e=np.exp(z)
    ss=float(e.sum())
    if ss<1e-300:
       e=np.full_like(e,1.0)
       ss=float(e.size)
    w=e/ss
    for ii in range(len(active)):
       k=int(active[ii]); tw=2.0*float(w[ii])
       if k<n:
          g[:k+1] += tw*a[:k+1][::-1]
       else:
          L=k-n+1
          g[L:n] += tw*a[n-1:L-1:-1]
    return g, mc
```
For K>=m active all, denom maybe weight_frac*mc; weights softmax over all, but if m large and K>=m (unlikely), fine. For K large, `np.argpartition(c,-K)` requires K int >0. Good.

`_lse_gradient`:
```
def _lse_gradient(a,c,tau_frac):
    n=len(a); mc=float(np.max(c))
    if mc<=1e-300: return np.zeros(n)
    denom=max(1e-300, mc*float(tau_frac))
    z=(c-mc)/denom
    z=z-np.max(z)
    z=np.clip(z,-50.0,0.0)
    p=np.exp(z); ps=float(p.sum())
    if ps<1e-300: p=np.ones_like(p); ps=float(p.size)
    else: p=p/ps
    N2=_next_power_of_two(3*n-2)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,N2)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N2)
    h=np.fft.irfft(P*np.conj(A),N2)
    h=h[:n]*2.0
    return h
```
Potential: `a` shape (n,), N2. Good.

`_blend_log`, `_perturb`, `_random_lowfreq`, `_analytic_init`, `_resample`.

`_random_lowfreq` uses math.pi.

`_perturb`:
```
def _perturb(a,snapshot,rng,strength):
    n=len(a); r=float(rng.random())
    if snapshot is not None and r < 0.35:
        return _blend_log(a, snapshot, float(rng.uniform(0.4,0.9)))
    if r < 0.75:
        b=a.copy()
        widths=[0.01,0.03,0.08,0.15,0.30]
        wf=float(rng.choice(widths))
        w=int(max(2,min(n,n*wf)))
        if n-w-1 < 1: s=0
        else: s=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
        if size: b[s:s+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,float(strength),size=w))
        return _normalize(b)
    else:
        b=a.copy()
        k=int(rng.integers(1,10))
        ph=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*math.pi))
        x=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
        b *= np.exp(0.5*float(strength)*np.sin(2.0*math.pi*k*x/n + ph))
        return _normalize(b)
```
If `strength` maybe 0.4, exp okay.

`_blend_log`:
```
def _blend_log(a,b,theta):
    ea=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); eb=np.log(np.maximum(b,1e-300))
    ec=theta*ea + (1.0-theta)*eb
    ec=ec-np.max(ec)
    return _normalize(np.exp(ec))
```

`_resample` as above.

`_analytic_init`:
```
def _analytic_init(n,rng):
    N1=_next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
    x=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
    best=_random_lowfreq(n,rng)
    best_obj=_obj(best,_conv_fft(best,N1))
    for k in (1,2,3,4,5):
      for alpha in (0.2,0.4,0.6,0.8):
        for ph in (0.0, math.pi/2.0, math.pi, 3.0*math.pi/2.0):
           f=1.0+alpha*np.sin(2.0*math.pi*k*x/n+ph)
           if float(np.min(f)) <= 0.02: continue
           a=_normalize(f); o=_obj(a,_conv_fft(a,N1))
           if o<best_obj: best,a? best=a; best_obj=o
    # two-term
    for k1 in (1,2,3):
      k2=k1+1
      for a1 in (0.3,0.5):
        for a2 in (0.1,0.2):
          for ph in (0.0,math.pi/2):
             f=1+a1*np.sin(2πk1x/n+ph)+a2*np.cos(2πk2x/n)
             if min<=0.02 continue
             eval
    return best
```
This may be slow ~5*4*4=80 + 3*2*2*2=24 =>104 convs. n=4096 conv ~0.2ms? 0.02s; n=8192 0.5ms =>0.05s. fine.

Maybe objective of pure sine with alpha=0.8 can be high; okay.

```
def _optimize_length(n, end_time, parent, init, tag='', smooth_frac=0.20):
    start_t=time.monotonic()
    end_time=float(end_time)
    total=end_time-start_t
    rng=np.random.default_rng(9999+n)
    if init is not None and len(init)==n:
       a=_normalize(init)
    elif parent is not None and len(parent)>0:
       a=_resample(parent,n)
       a *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.01,size=n))
       a=_normalize(a)
    else:
       a=_analytic_init(n,rng)
    N1=_next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
    c=_conv_fft(a,N1)
    cur_o=_obj(a,c)
    best=a.copy(); best_o=cur_o; best_c=c.copy(); snapshot=best.copy()
    if total < 30:
       print(...)
       return best,best_o
    phase1_start=start_t
    phase1_end=start_t+smooth_frac*max(0.0,total)
    active_start=phase1_end
    eta=0.05
    it=0; no_improve=0
    last_improve=start_t; last_print=start_t; last_snapshot=start_t; last_lse=start_t
    if phase1_end > start_t:
       print(f"[{tag}] start n={n} obj={cur_o:.6f}")
    while time.monotonic() < end_time - 2.0:
       now=time.monotonic()
       if cur_o > best_o + 1e-7 and no_improve > 80:
          a=best.copy(); c=best_c.copy(); cur_o=best_o
       # gradient
       if now < phase1_end:
          frac=0.0
          if phase1_end > start_t:
             frac=min(1.0,(now-start_t)/(phase1_end-start_t))
          tau_frac=0.18 - 0.13*frac
          g=_lse_gradient(a,c,tau_frac)
          use_log=(it % 8 == 0)
          last_lse=now
       else:
          use_lse = (now - last_lse > 8.0) or (no_improve > 3000)
          if use_lse:
             g=_lse_gradient(a,c,0.012)
             last_lse=now
             use_log=(it % 8 == 0)
          else:
             rem=end_time-active_start
             af=0.0
             if rem > 0:
                af=min(1.0,(now-active_start)/rem)
             if af<0.25: K=96; wt=0.025
             elif af<0.55: K=64; wt=0.012
             elif af<0.85: K=32; wt=0.006
             else: K=16; wt=0.003
             g, _ = _active_gradient(a,c,K,wt)
             use_log = (it % 7 == 0) or (no_improve > 800)
       it += 1
       # quick gmax
       gc_tmp = g - g.mean()
       gmax_tmp = float(np.max(np.abs(gc_tmp)))
       if gmax_tmp < 1e-18:
          strength=0.15
          a=_perturb(best,snapshot,rng,strength)
          c=_conv_fft(a,N1); cur_o=_obj(a,c)
          eta=0.05; no_improve=0; last_improve=now
          continue
       # stall/perturb
       if now - last_improve > 45.0:
          strength = 0.08 + 0.30*min(1.0, max(0.0, (now-last_improve-45.0))/120.0)
          if rng.random() < 0.10:
              a=_random_lowfreq(n,rng)
          else:
              a=_perturb(best,snapshot,rng,strength)
          c=_conv_fft(a,N1); cur_o=_obj(a,c)
          if cur_o < best_o - 1e-11:
              best_o=cur_o; best=a.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); last_improve=now
              if now-last_snapshot>30: snapshot=best.copy(); last_snapshot=now
          else:
              # reset last_improve to now? If we do, next perturb in 45s.
              last_improve=now
          eta=0.05; no_improve=0
          continue
       accepted,a2,c2,o2,eta,kind,te=_line_search(a,c,n,N1,g,eta,use_log)
       if accepted:
          a=a2; c=c2; cur_o=o2
          no_improve=0
          if cur_o < best_o - 1e-11:
             best_o=cur_o; best=a.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); last_improve=time.monotonic()
             if time.monotonic() - last_snapshot > 30.0:
                snapshot=best.copy(); last_snapshot=time.monotonic()
       else:
          no_improve += 1
          if cur_o > best_o + 1e-7 and no_improve > 80:
             a=best.copy(); c=best_c.copy(); cur_o=best_o
       if time.monotonic() - last_print > 5.0:
          print(f"[{tag}] it={it} obj={cur_o:.6f} best={best_o:.6f} eta={eta:.3f}")
          last_print=time.monotonic()
    # final maybe one exact? no
    return best,best_o
```
One issue: In stall, if we set `last_improve=now` even when perturb worsens, we delay next stall 45s. That's intended. But if perturb doesn't improve, no_improve reset and current maybe worse; line search from current could take time. If no improvement for 45s, perturb again. Good.

`strength` formula uses now-last_improve after 45, but we set last_improve before? In condition `now - last_improve >45`, compute strength based on overage; after set last_improve=now. Good.

If perturbation uses `_random_lowfreq`, it may be all positive but not related; okay.

Could `_perturb` call `_random_lowfreq`? No, separate.

Maybe in stall, if current best is much better, we ignore current. Good.

Potential performance: `gc_tmp = g - g.mean()` then line_search recomputes same; duplicate O(n). We can modify line_search to accept precomputed gc/gmax? Simpler duplicate okay but could be costly. We can in `_line_search` recompute; n~5000, O(5k) negligible vs FFT. Fine.

def propose_candidate():
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+2380.0

          parent=np.maximum(parent,0.0)

          parent=None

    s=float(arr.sum())
    if s<1e-12: arr=np.full(len(arr) if arr.size else 1,1.0)/...; 
    else: arr=arr/s
    if arr.size ==0: return [1.0]
    return [float(x) for x in arr]

```python
"""
Three-episode hybrid optimizer for minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
over nonnegative step-height sequences.

The optimizer:
- starts from the provided parent sequence when available, otherwise uses
  analytic low-frequency initializations,
- uses fast FFT convolutions for objective and active-set subgradients,
- alternates between a smoothed LSE active-gradient phase and a sharper
  top-K max-convolution active-set phase,
- performs backtracking line searches, occasional log-parameter updates,
  snapshot blending and broader random/low-frequency restarts,
- searches two related lengths and then refines the best length further.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math


def _next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.full(a.shape, 1.0 / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def _conv_fft(a, N):
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N)
    m = 2 * len(a) - 1
    c = c[:m]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def _obj(a, c):
    n = len(a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    return 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c)) / (s * s)


def _active_gradient(a, c, K, weight_frac):
    n = len(a)
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    if mc <= 1e-300:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64), 0.0

    m = len(c)
    K = int(K)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if K <= 1:
        k = int(np.argmax(c))
        tw = 2.0
        if k < n:
            g[:k + 1] += tw * a[:k + 1][::-1]
        else:
            L = k - n + 1
            g[L:n] += tw * a[n - 1:L - 1:-1]
        return g, mc

    if K >= m:
        active = np.arange(m)
    else:
        active = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]

    ca = c[active]
    denom = max(1e-300, mc * float(weight_frac))
    z = (ca - mc) / denom
    z = z - np.max(z)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    ss = float(e.sum())
    if ss < 1e-300:
        e = np.full_like(e, 1.0)
        ss = float(e.size)
    w = e / ss

    for ii in range(len(active)):
        k = int(active[ii])
        tw = 2.0 * float(w[ii])
        if k < n:
            g[:k + 1] += tw * a[:k + 1][::-1]
        else:
            L = k - n + 1
            g[L:n] += tw * a[n - 1:L - 1:-1]

    return g, mc


def _lse_gradient(a, c, tau_frac):
    n = len(a)
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    if mc <= 1e-300:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    denom = max(1e-300, mc * float(tau_frac))
    z = (c - mc) / denom
    z = z - np.max(z)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    p = np.exp(z)
    ps = float(p.sum())
    if ps < 1e-300:
        p = np.ones_like(p, dtype=np.float64)
        ps = float(p.size)
    else:
        p = p / ps

    N2 = _next_power_of_two(3 * n - 2)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N2)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N2)
    h = np.fft.irfft(P * np.conj(A), N2)
    h = h[:n] * 2.0
    return h


def _blend_log(a, b, theta):
    ea = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    eb = np.log(np.maximum(b, 1e-300))
    ec = theta * ea + (1.0 - theta) * eb
    ec = ec - np.max(ec)
    return _normalize(np.exp(ec))


def _perturb(a, snapshot, rng, strength):
    n = len(a)
    r = float(rng.random())

    if snapshot is not None and r < 0.35:
        return _blend_log(a, snapshot, float(rng.uniform(0.4, 0.9)))

    if r < 0.75:
        b = a.copy()
        widths = [0.01, 0.03, 0.08, 0.15, 0.30]
        wf = float(rng.choice(widths))
        w = int(max(2, min(n, n * wf)))
        if n - w - 1 < 1:
            s = 0
        else:
            s = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
        b[s:s + w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(strength), size=w))
        return _normalize(b)

    b = a.copy()
    k = int(rng.integers(1, 10))
    ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b *= np.exp(0.5 * float(strength) * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * k * x / float(n) + ph))
    return _normalize(b)


def _random_lowfreq(n, rng):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    for _ in range(5):
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, 8))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.25))
        f += amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * freq * x / float(n) + phase)

    f *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, size=n))
    f = np.maximum(f, 1e-3)
    return _normalize(f)


def _analytic_init(n, rng):
    N1 = _next_power_of_two(2 * n - 1)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)

    best = _random_lowfreq(n, rng)
    best_obj = _obj(best, _conv_fft(best, N1))

    phases = (0.0, math.pi / 2.0, math.pi, 3.0 * math.pi / 2.0)

    for k in (1, 2, 3, 4, 5):
        for alpha in (0.2, 0.4, 0.6, 0.8):
            for ph in phases:
                f = 1.0 + alpha * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * k * x / float(n) + ph)
                if float(np.min(f)) <= 0.02:
                    continue
                a = _normalize(f)
                o = _obj(a, _conv_fft(a, N1))
                if o < best_obj:
                    best_obj = o
                    best = a

    for k1 in (1, 2, 3):
        k2 = k1 + 1
        for a1 in (0.3, 0.5):
            for a2 in (0.1, 0.2):
                for ph in (0.0, math.pi / 2.0):
                    f = (
                        1.0
                        + a1 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * k1 * x / float(n) + ph)
                        + a2 * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * k2 * x / float(n))
                    )
                    if float(np.min(f)) <= 0.02:
                        continue
                    a = _normalize(f)
                    o = _obj(a, _conv_fft(a, N1))
                    if o < best_obj:
                        best_obj = o
                        best = a

    return best


def _resample(parent, n):
    if parent is None:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)

    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    if p.size == 1:
        a = np.full(n, float(p[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        a = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)

    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    return _normalize(a)


def _line_search(a, c, n, N, g, eta, use_log):
    cur_o = _obj(a, c)
    gc = g - g.mean()
    gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(gc)))
    if gmax < 1e-18:
        return False, a, c, cur_o, max(eta * 0.75, 1e-5), "none", 0.0

    mean_a = float(a.mean())
    dirv = gc * (mean_a / gmax)

    # Additive projection-like update.
    for te in (eta, 0.5 * eta, 0.25 * eta):
        b = a - te * dirv
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
            continue
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        s = float(b.sum())
        if s < 1e-12:
            continue
        b = b / s
        c2 = _conv_fft(b, N)
        o2 = _obj(b, c2)
        if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < cur_o - 1e-11:
            return True, b, c2, o2, min(0.30, te * 1.05), "add", te

    if use_log:
        h = gc / gmax
        h = np.clip(h, -2.0, 2.0)
        for te in (eta, 0.5 * eta, 0.25 * eta):
            b = a * np.exp(-te * h)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
                continue
            s = float(b.sum())
            if s < 1e-12:
                continue
            b = b / s
            c2 = _conv_fft(b, N)
            o2 = _obj(b, c2)
            if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < cur_o - 1e-11:
                return True, b, c2, o2, min(0.30, te * 1.05), "log", te

    return False, a, c, cur_o, max(eta * 0.75, 1e-5), "none", 0.0


def _optimize_length(n, end_time, parent, init, tag="", smooth_frac=0.20):
    start_t = time.monotonic()
    end_time = float(end_time)
    total = end_time - start_t

    rng = np.random.default_rng(9871 + int(n))

    if init is not None and len(init) == n:
        a = _normalize(init)
    elif parent is not None and len(parent) > 0:
        a = _resample(parent, n)
        a *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.01, size=n))
        a = _normalize(a)
    else:
        a = _analytic_init(n, rng)

    N1 = _next_power_of_two(2 * n - 1)
    c = _conv_fft(a, N1)
    cur_o = _obj(a, c)

    best = a.copy()
    best_o = cur_o
    best_c = c.copy()
    snapshot = best.copy()

    print(f"[{tag}] init n={n} obj={best_o:.6f}")

    if total < 30.0:
        return best, best_o

    phase1_start = start_t
    phase1_end = start_t + smooth_frac * max(0.0, total)
    active_start = phase1_end

    eta = 0.05
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    last_improve = start_t
    last_print = start_t
    last_snapshot = start_t
    last_lse = start_t

    while time.monotonic() < end_time - 2.0:
        now = time.monotonic()

        if cur_o > best_o + 1e-7 and no_improve > 80:
            a = best.copy()
            c = best_c.copy()
            cur_o = best_o

        if now < phase1_end:
            frac = 0.0
            if phase1_end > phase1_start:
                frac = min(1.0, (now - phase1_start) / (phase1_end - phase1_start))
            tau_frac = 0.18 - 0.13 * frac
            g = _lse_gradient(a, c, tau_frac)
            use_log = (it % 8 == 0)
            last_lse = now
        else:
            if (now - last_lse > 8.0) or (no_improve > 3000):
                g = _lse_gradient(a, c, 0.012)
                last_lse = now
                use_log = (it % 8 == 0)
            else:
                rem = end_time - active_start
                af = 0.0
                if rem > 0.0:
                    af = min(1.0, (now - active_start) / rem)

                if af < 0.25:
                    K = 96
                    wt = 0.025
                elif af < 0.55:
                    K = 64
                    wt = 0.012
                elif af < 0.85:
                    K = 32
                    wt = 0.006
                else:
                    K = 16
                    wt = 0.003

                g, _ = _active_gradient(a, c, K, wt)
                use_log = (it % 7 == 0) or (no_improve > 800)

        it += 1

        gc_chk = g - g.mean()
        gmax_chk = float(np.max(np.abs(gc_chk)))
        if gmax_chk < 1e-18:
            strength = 0.15
            a = _perturb(best, snapshot, rng, strength)
            c = _conv_fft(a, N1)
            cur_o = _obj(a, c)
            eta = 0.05
            no_improve = 0
            last_improve = now
            continue

        if now - last_improve > 45.0:
            overage = min(1.0, max(0.0, (now - last_improve - 45.0) / 120.0))
            strength = 0.08 + 0.30 * overage

            if float(rng.random()) < 0.10:
                a = _random_lowfreq(n, rng)
            else:
                a = _perturb(best, snapshot, rng, strength)

            c = _conv_fft(a, N1)
            cur_o = _obj(a, c)

            if cur_o < best_o - 1e-11:
                best_o = cur_o
                best = a.copy()
                best_c = c.copy()
                last_improve = time.monotonic()
                if time.monotonic() - last_snapshot > 30.0:
                    snapshot = best.copy()
                    last_snapshot = time.monotonic()
            else:
                last_improve = time.monotonic()

            eta = 0.05
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        accepted, a2, c2, o2, eta_new, kind, te = _line_search(
            a, c, n, N1, g, eta, use_log
        )

        if accepted:
            a = a2
            c = c2
            cur_o = o2
            eta = eta_new
            no_improve = 0
            if cur_o < best_o - 1e-11:
                best_o = cur_o
                best = a.copy()
                best_c = c.copy()
                last_improve = time.monotonic()
                if time.monotonic() - last_snapshot > 30.0:
                    snapshot = best.copy()
                    last_snapshot = time.monotonic()
        else:
            eta = eta_new
            no_improve += 1
            if cur_o > best_o + 1e-7 and no_improve > 80:
                a = best.copy()
                c = best_c.copy()
                cur_o = best_o

        if time.monotonic() - last_print > 5.0:
            print(f"[{tag}] it={it} obj={cur_o:.6f} best={best_o:.6f} eta={eta:.3f}")
            last_print = time.monotonic()

    print(f"[{tag}] done obj={best_o:.6f}")
    return best, best_o


def _safe_score(seq):
    try:
        if seq is None:
            return float("inf")
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.size == 0:
            return float("inf")
        lst = [float(x) for x in arr]
        return float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def propose_candidate():
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + 2380.0

    parent = None
    try:
        parent = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        try:
            parent = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64)
            parent = np.nan_to_num(parent, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            parent = np.maximum(parent, 0.0)
        except Exception:
            parent = None

    rng = np.random.default_rng(20240524)

    if parent is not None and parent.size >= 512:
        nA = int(parent.size)
    else:
        nA = 4096

    MIN_N = 2048
    MAX_N = 10000
    nA = int(max(MIN_N, min(MAX_N, nA)))

    nB = int(round(nA * 1.35 / 64.0) * 64)
    if nB <= nA:
        nB = min(MAX_N, nA + 512)
    if nB > MAX_N:
        nB = MAX_N

    total = deadline - start
    t1 = start + 0.35 * total
    t2 = start + 0.70 * total

    print(f"Length plan: A={nA}, B={nB}")

    bestA, objA = _optimize_length(nA, t1, parent, None, "A", 0.20)

    scoreA = float("inf")
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 300.0:
        scoreA = _safe_score(bestA)

    initB = _resample(bestA, nB)
    if nB != nA:
        initB = _perturb(initB, initB, rng, 0.15)
    else:
        initB = _perturb(initB, initB, rng, 0.25)

    bestB, objB = _optimize_length(nB, t2, parent, initB, "B", 0.12)

    scoreB = float("inf")
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
        scoreB = _safe_score(bestB)

    useB = False
    if scoreA < float("inf") and scoreB < float("inf"):
        useB = scoreB < scoreA
    else:
        useB = objB < objA

    if useB:
        nC = nB
        initC = bestB
    else:
        nC = nA
        initC = bestA

    t3 = deadline - 25.0
    if time.monotonic() < t3 - 60.0:
        bestC, objC = _optimize_length(nC, t3, parent, initC, "C", 0.06)
    else:
        bestC = initC
        objC = objB if useB else objA

    candidates = [bestA, bestB, bestC]
    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
        candidates.append(parent)

    best_seq = bestC
    best_score = float("inf")
    for seq in candidates:
        if seq is None:
            continue
        sc = _safe_score(seq)
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = seq

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = np.ones(nA, dtype=np.float64)

    arr = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 1e-300)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        arr = np.ones(max(1, arr.size), dtype=np.float64)
        s = 1.0
    arr = arr / s

    if arr.size == 0:
        return [1.0]

    print(f"FINAL returned score({best_score:.6f}) length={arr.size}")
    return [float(x) for x in arr]
```
